# Guanzhi 官制 - 动态头衔官职授予效果 (生成自 _build/make_offices.py)
# 共享选人: root=君主 -> scope:guanzhi_appointee
# 两个写死版本, 避免 $KIND$ 名字替换失效导致过滤不生效
guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_councillor = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_councillor = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_courtier = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_courtier = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_vassal = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_vassal = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_civil_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
}

guanzhi_find_office_candidate_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_councillor = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_councillor = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_courtier = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_courtier = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_vassal = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
		}
		ordered_vassal = {
			order_by = guanzhi_appointment_score_value
			limit = {
				guanzhi_office_candidate_trigger = { LIEGE = scope:guanzhi_liege }
			}
			position = 0
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_appointee
		}
	}
}


# ---- 太师 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_taishi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_taishi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_taishi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_taishi
		set_coa = k_gz_t_taishi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_taishi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taishi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taishi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_01 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 太师 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_taishi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taishi
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_taishi: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_taishi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_taishi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_taishi
		set_coa = k_gz_t_taishi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_taishi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taishi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taishi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_01 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_coa = k_gz_t_taibao
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_taibao value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taibao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taibao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_01 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 太尉 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_taiwei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_taiwei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_taiwei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taiwei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taiwei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_01 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 太尉 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_taiwei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taiwei
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_taiwei: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_taiwei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_taiwei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_taiwei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taiwei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taiwei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_01 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_shangshuling
		set_coa = k_gz_t_shangshuling
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_shangshuling value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_shangshuling
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shangshuling
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_02 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 尚书令 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_shangshuling_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shangshuling
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_shangshuling: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_shangshuling
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_shangshuling
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_shangshuling
		set_coa = k_gz_t_shangshuling
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_shangshuling value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_shangshuling
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shangshuling
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_02 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 尚书左仆射 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_zuopuye_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_zuopuye
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_zuopuye
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_zuopuye
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuopuye
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuopuye value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuopuye
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuopuye
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_zhongshuling
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zhongshuling
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zhongshuling value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zhongshuling
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zhongshuling
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 侍中 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_shizhong_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		set_coa = k_gz_t_shizhong
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_shizhong value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_shizhong
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shizhong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 侍中 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_shizhong_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shizhong
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_shizhong: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_shizhong
		set_coa = k_gz_t_shizhong
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_shizhong value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_shizhong
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_shizhong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_libu_rites
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_libu_rites
		set_coa = k_gz_t_libu_rites
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_libu_rites value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_libu_rites
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_libu_rites
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 礼部侍郎 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_libu_rites_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_libu_rites
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_libu_rites: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_libu_rites
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_libu_rites
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_libu_rites
		set_coa = k_gz_t_libu_rites
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_libu_rites value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_libu_rites
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_libu_rites
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 兵部侍郎 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_bingbu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_bingbu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_bingbu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_bingbu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_bingbu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_bingbu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_bingbu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_bingbu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_gongbu: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = gz_institution.t_gongbu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = gz_institution.t_gongbu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = gz_institution.t_gongbu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_gongbu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_gongbu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_gongbu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_gongbu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 御史大夫 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_yushidafu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_yushidafu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_yushidafu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_yushidafu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 御史大夫 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_yushidafu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_yushidafu
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_yushidafu: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_yushidafu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_yushidafu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_yushidafu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_yushidafu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 卫尉寺卿 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_weiwei_si_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		set_coa = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_weiwei_si value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_weiwei_si
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_weiwei_si
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 卫尉寺卿 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_weiwei_si_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_weiwei_si
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_weiwei_si: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		set_coa = k_gz_t_weiwei_si
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_weiwei_si value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_weiwei_si
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_weiwei_si
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 大理寺卿 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_dali_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_dali
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_dali: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_dali
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_dali
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_dali
		set_coa = k_gz_t_dali
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_dali value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_dali
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_dali
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 鸿胪寺卿 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_honglu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_honglu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_honglu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_honglu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_honglu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_honglu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_honglu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_honglu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_taifu_si
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_taifu_si
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 国子监祭酒 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_guozi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_guozi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_guozi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_guozi
		set_coa = d_gz_t_guozi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_guozi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_guozi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_guozi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 国子监祭酒 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_guozi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_guozi
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_guozi: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_guozi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_guozi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_guozi
		set_coa = d_gz_t_guozi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_guozi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_guozi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_guozi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_junqi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_junqi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_06 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 军器监 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_junqi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_junqi
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_junqi: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_junqi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_junqi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_junqi
		set_coa = d_gz_t_junqi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_junqi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_junqi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_junqi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_06 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 都水监 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_dushui_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_dushui
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_dushui
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_dushui
		set_coa = d_gz_t_dushui
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_dushui value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change

		}

	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_dushui
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_dushui
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title

	}

	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_dianzhong
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_dianzhong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_05 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 内侍监 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_neishi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_neishi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_neishi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_neishi
		set_coa = d_gz_t_neishi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_neishi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_neishi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_neishi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 内侍监 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_neishi_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_neishi
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_neishi: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_neishi
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_t_neishi
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_neishi
		set_coa = d_gz_t_neishi
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_neishi value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_neishi
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_neishi
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wen_04 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuoxiaowei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuoxiaowei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuoxiaowei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 左骁卫大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_zuoxiaowei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuoxiaowei
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_zuoxiaowei: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuoxiaowei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuoxiaowei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuoxiaowei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuoxiaowei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 右骁卫大将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_youxiaowei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_youxiaowei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_youxiaowei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_youxiaowei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_youxiaowei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youxiaowei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change

			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youxiaowei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youxiaowei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 右武卫大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_youwuwei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youwuwei
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_youwuwei: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_youwuwei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_youwuwei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_youwuwei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_youwuwei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youwuwei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youwuwei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youwuwei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 左威卫大将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_zuoweiwei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_zuoweiwei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_zuoweiwei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_zuoweiwei
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuoweiwei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuoweiwei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuoweiwei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuoweiwei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

		generate_coa = yes
