		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuolingjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuolingjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuolingjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuolingjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 右领军卫大将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_youlingjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		set_coa = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youlingjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youlingjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youlingjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 右领军卫大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_youlingjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youlingjun
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_youlingjun: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		set_coa = k_gz_t_youlingjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youlingjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youlingjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youlingjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 左监门卫大将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_t_zuojianmen_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuojianmen value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuojianmen
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuojianmen
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 左监门卫大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_zuojianmen_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuojianmen
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_zuojianmen: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		set_coa = k_gz_t_zuojianmen
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_zuojianmen value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_zuojianmen
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_zuojianmen
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }

		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youqianniu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youqianniu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youqianniu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 右千牛卫大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_t_youqianniu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youqianniu
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick t_youqianniu: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_t_youqianniu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_t_youqianniu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_t_youqianniu
		set_coa = k_gz_t_youqianniu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_t_youqianniu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_tang_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_t_youqianniu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_t_youqianniu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
		guanzhi_san_auto_grant_wu_02 = yes
		guanzhi_xun_auto_grant_03 = yes
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 丞相 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_chengxiang_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		set_coa = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_chengxiang value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_chengxiang
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_chengxiang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 丞相 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_chengxiang_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_chengxiang
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_chengxiang: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_chengxiang
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county

		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title

		}
		generate_coa = yes

		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_chengxiang value = yes }

	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {

			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}

			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_chengxiang
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_chengxiang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title

	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"

		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_yushidafu
		set_coa = k_gz_h_yushidafu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_yushidafu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_yushidafu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_yushidafu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 太常 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_taichang_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_taichang
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_taichang
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_taichang
		set_coa = k_gz_h_taichang
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_taichang value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_taichang
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taichang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 太常 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_taichang_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taichang
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_taichang: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_taichang
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_taichang
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_taichang
		set_coa = k_gz_h_taichang
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_taichang value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_taichang
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taichang
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_taipu
		set_coa = k_gz_h_taipu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_taipu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_taipu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taipu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 太仆 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_taipu_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taipu
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_taipu: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_taipu
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_taipu
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_taipu
		set_coa = k_gz_h_taipu
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_taipu value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_taipu
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_taipu
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 廷尉 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_tingwei_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_tingwei
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_tingwei
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_tingwei
		set_coa = k_gz_h_tingwei
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_tingwei value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_tingwei
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_tingwei
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_zongzheng
		set_coa = k_gz_h_zongzheng
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_zongzheng value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_zongzheng
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_zongzheng
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 大司农 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_dasinong_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		set_coa = k_gz_h_dasinong
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_dasinong value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_dasinong
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dasinong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 大司农 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_dasinong_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dasinong
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_dasinong: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_dasinong
		set_coa = k_gz_h_dasinong
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_dasinong value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_dasinong
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dasinong
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_coa = d_gz_h_shangshupuye
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_shangshupuye value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_shangshupuye
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_shangshupuye
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 尚书仆射 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_shangshupuye_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_shangshupuye
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_shangshupuye: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_shangshupuye
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_h_shangshupuye
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_shangshupuye
		set_coa = d_gz_h_shangshupuye
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_shangshupuye value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_shangshupuye
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_shangshupuye
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 吏曹尚书 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_licao_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_licao
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_h_licao
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_licao
		set_coa = d_gz_h_licao
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_licao value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_licao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_licao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 吏曹尚书 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_licao_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_licao
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_licao: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_licao
		}
	}

			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_h_licao
		}

		add_claim_on_loss = no

	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {

			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege

		}

			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}

	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_licao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_licao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_mincao
		set_coa = d_gz_h_mincao
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_mincao value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_mincao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_mincao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 三公曹尚书 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_sangongcao_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		set_coa = d_gz_h_sangongcao
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_sangongcao value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_sangongcao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_sangongcao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 三公曹尚书 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_sangongcao_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_sangongcao
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_civil_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_sangongcao: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = duchy
			name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_sangongcao
		set_coa = d_gz_h_sangongcao
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_civil value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_sangongcao value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_civil_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_sangongcao
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_sangongcao
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_civil
		set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_civil_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_dajiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_dajiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dajiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 大将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_dajiangjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dajiangjun
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_dajiangjun: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_dajiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_dajiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_dajiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_dajiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 骠骑将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_piaoji_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_piaoji
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_piaoji
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_piaoji
		set_coa = k_gz_h_piaoji
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_piaoji value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {

			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_piaoji
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_piaoji
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_weijiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_weijiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_weijiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_weijiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_weijiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_weijiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

# ---- 前将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_qianjiangjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_qianjiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_qianjiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_qianjiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 前将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_qianjiangjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_qianjiangjun
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_qianjiangjun: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_qianjiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_qianjiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_qianjiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_qianjiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

	}
}

# ---- 右将军 授予(菜单) ----
guanzhi_grant_office_h_youjiangjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
			destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:recipient
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_youjiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_youjiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_youjiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
}

# ---- 右将军 任命(自动选人) ----
guanzhi_appoint_office_h_youjiangjun_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guanzhi_liege
	# 已有人在任则跳过
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_youjiangjun
				}
			}
		}
		guanzhi_find_office_candidate_military_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:guanzhi_appointee }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI pick h_youjiangjun: [guanzhi_appointee.GetShortUIName]"
	if = {
		limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_hegemony }
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = empire
			name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_dynamic_title = {
			tier = kingdom
			name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = guanzhi_give_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:new_title = {
		set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_color_from_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_capital_county = scope:guanzhi_liege.capital_county
		set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:guanzhi_liege.primary_title
		set_landless_title = yes
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:guanzhi_appointee
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
		generate_coa = yes
		set_title_name = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		set_coa = k_gz_h_youjiangjun
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_is_office value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_h_youjiangjun value = yes }
		add_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law
	}
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		change_liege = {
			liege = scope:guanzhi_liege
			change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:guanzhi_give_change
	scope:new_title = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: office var MISSING"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_title_law = guanzhi_han_military_appointment_succession_law }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law SET"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI diag: law MISSING"
		}
	}
	# 官职名 trait + 朝官公共 trait + 角色旗标(供风味化顶掉黜陟使)
	scope:guanzhi_appointee = {
		add_trait = guanzhi_zhiguan_h_youjiangjun
		add_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_h_youjiangjun
		add_character_flag = guanzhi_office_military

		if = {
			limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
			set_primary_title_to = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = guanzhi_office_military_title
			value = scope:new_title
		}
	}
	debug_log = "GUANZHI office granted"
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = { name = guanzhi_office_military value = yes }

# 罢免朝官: 销毁其全部朝官头衔并除去官职 trait 与旗标. scope=被罢免者
guanzhi_revoke_offices_effect = {
	debug_log = "GUANZHI revoke offices called"
	every_held_title = {
		limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_office }
		destroy_title = this
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title }
		destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_civil_title
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title }
		destroy_title = this.var:guanzhi_office_military_title
	}
	guanzhi_remove_office_trait_effect = yes
	guanzhi_clear_office_flags_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { guanzhi_holds_office_trigger = yes } }
		remove_trait = guanzhi_chaoguan
	}
}
